營收走勢圖

玻璃陶瓷–電子材料  
富喬(1815.TT) 
AI 材料升級趨勢確立，高階玻纖布開啟成長新局

富喬為台灣少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方能力的玻纖布廠。2H26 
受惠二代低介電布放量、報價調漲及良率改善，營收與 獲利可望逐季創
高；進入 2027 年後，獲利成長動能將由高階產品占比提升與新增產能
貢獻。台灣廠持續將既有產能轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起分
批將 E 布產能升級至二代布，泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐步投產，承接
客戶對非中國高階產能的需求，加上低膨脹布開始導入，產品組合將進
一步往高階化發展。研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 
5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後淨利分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富喬
為日系龍頭以外少數具備垂直整合與自有配方能力的供應商，以  2027 
年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買進
投資評等。

1. 規格升級推動產品組合高階化 
AI 伺服器與高速交換器的傳輸速率持續提升 ， 板材由 M7、M8 向 M9 以
上升級，玻纖布同步由 E-glass 轉向一代、二代低介電布。石英布因鑽
孔與除膠渣良率受限而導入遞延，終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布，延
長其產品週期。研究部預估富喬二代低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%。

2. 既有機台重新配置，毛利率大幅跳升 
玻纖布織布機由豐田獨家供應 ， 新機交期已排至 2031 年，2027 年以前
產業新增供給主要仰賴既有機台重新配置 。 富喬現有約 920 台織機 ， 憑
藉紗、布垂直整合，可在無須大幅增加資本支出下將既有產能轉向二代
低介電布 ， 帶動毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%， 升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 
2027 年的 54.8%。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 2

公司簡介 
富喬工業（1815 TT）成立於 1999 年，為台灣少數具備玻璃纖維紗至玻璃纖
維布垂直整合能力的材料廠，主要產品涵蓋電子級、工業級玻纖紗及電子級玻
纖布，其中電子級玻纖布為銅箔基板（CCL）與印刷電路板（PCB）的關鍵上游
材料。公司目前主要生產基地位於雲林斗六、虎尾及中國東莞，並已布局泰國
新產能，以強化非中國供應鏈配置。相較單純織布廠，富喬具備自有玻纖紗生
產與配方研發能力，可依終端規格變化調整紗種與產品組合。近年受惠 AI 伺
服器、高速交換器等應用帶動 PCB 材料規格持續升級，富喬營運重心逐步由
傳統 E-glass 轉向低介電等高階電子級材料。

玻纖布產業介紹 
玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最上游，製程橫跨玻璃熔煉與紡織兩個領域。玻
纖原料經窯爐熔融後 ， 由鉑銠漏板拉製成單絲 ， 再經上漿 、 集束與加撚形成電
子紗 ， 後續以織機織成玻纖布 ， 並經開纖及矽烷偶聯劑等後處理後 ， 交由 CCL 
廠含浸樹脂製成膠片，再與銅箔壓合成銅箔基板，最終應用於 PCB 或載板。 
玻纖布規格主要可由織物型號 、 紗徑與玻璃配方三個面向區分 。 以織物型號而
言，7628 屬厚布，基重約 210 g/m²，2116、1080 依序變薄，10 系列則屬超
薄布；若依紗徑區分，粗紗單纖直徑約 9 至 13 微米，細紗則降至約 4 至 
9 微米，隨紗徑變細，斷絲風險與製程難度同步提高，織機亦須降速，超薄布
生產效率僅約厚布的二分之一至三分之一 。 另一方面 ， 玻璃配方則直接決定材
料電性 ， 介電損耗亦隨材料世代持續下降 。 隨高速傳輸規格提升 ，玻纖與樹脂
之間的介電差異會放大訊號時延與織紋效應 ， 因此傳輸速率每提升一個世代 ，
材料亦須同步升級。 
除玻璃配方外，織布後的開纖與表面處理亦是高階玻纖布性能的關鍵。即使
CCL 廠商採用相同原料，不同玻纖布廠的產品在電性、耐熱性與良率上的表現
仍可能存在顯著差異，也使高階玻纖布認證週期通常長達兩年以上。就材料成
本結構而言，玻纖布占傳統 CCL 材料成本約 25% 至 40%，若升級至石英布，
占比可提高至約六成；隨高階 PCB 對 CCL 規格與用量提升，CCL 占 PCB 用
料成本比重亦由過去三至四成提升至五成以上。玻纖布因此已由過去的通用材
料，逐步轉變為影響高速板材性能、成本與供應穩定性的關鍵材料。

富喬為台灣少數具備玻纖紗、布
垂直整合與自有配方能力的材料
廠，近年受惠 AI 帶動高階 PCB 
材料升級 ， 營運重心正由傳統 E-
glass 轉向低介電玻纖布，並同
步布局泰國非中國產能。

玻纖布位於 PCB 材料供應鏈最
上游 ， 製程與認證門檻高 ， 且隨高
速傳輸推升材料規格升級，其成
本占比與對板材性能的重要性同
步提升，已由通用材料轉為高階  
PCB 的關鍵材料。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

AI 世代推升高速材料規格持續升級 
AI 基礎建設對材料的需求，並非僅來自伺服器出貨量成長，更重要的是運算
密度與傳輸速率持續提升，帶動 PCB 材料規格同步升級。相較傳統伺服器約
三至四年的平台週期，AI 伺服器迭代速度明顯加快，GPU、ASIC 與交換器間
的資料傳輸需求快速增加，推動網路架構由 400G、800G 向 1.6T、3.2T 演
進。隨訊號速率提升，傳輸損耗與訊號完整性要求同步提高，板材須持續降低
介電常數與介電損耗，帶動 CCL 由 M7、M8 向 M9 以上升級；玻纖布作為影
響板材電性的重要材料，亦由傳統 E-glass 逐步升級至一代、二代低介電
布，並進一步朝石英布與第三代低介電材料發展。 
就目前材料演進路徑而言，400G 交換器主要採用 M7 板材搭配一代低介電
布，800G 升級至 M8 後，一代與二代低介電布開始並用；進入 1.6T 世代
後，二代低介電布的重要性進一步提升，部分高階規格則開始評估導入石英
布。展望 2028 年後，隨 3.2T 逐步導入，材料規格預期將進一步朝升級版石
英布或第三代低介電布發展，高階玻纖布的滲透率持續提升。 
研究部認為，規格升級將成為本輪玻纖布產業成長的核心驅動力。隨 AI 運算
與高速傳輸規格持續演進，材料需求將由傳統 E-glass 加速轉向低介電、低
膨脹等高階產品。在終端平台持續更替的背景下，玻纖布須隨板材世代同步升
級，而每一次規格提升亦伴隨 ASP 與單位產值增加，使產業成長不僅來自需
求量擴張，更來自產品組合持續高階化。

表二、CCL 等級與對應玻纖布

資料來源：統一投顧整理

升，帶動 PCB 材料由 E-glass 
向低介電、低膨脹等高階玻纖布
升級，推升高階產品滲透率、ASP 
與單位產值，成為產業成長的核
心驅動力。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

專利與認證構成高階玻纖布的實質進入障礙 
高階玻纖布的核心技術門檻在玻纖紗配方，低介電與低膨脹材料的關鍵專利亦
主要集中於紗端，後進者若要切入，須重新設計配方以迴避既有專利，良率與
量產時程因而受限。另一方面，CCL 廠需將特定玻纖布與樹脂、銅箔共同調
校，一旦納入合格供應商名單，更換材料通常須重新開立料號並進行完整驗
證，使認證週期可長達數年。富喬憑藉紗、布垂直整合，可直接由紗端調整配
方與製程，並已完成多地高階低介電玻纖布專利布局，低膨脹布亦已突破既有
專利限制並通過終端認證。研究部認為專利保護與長認證週期共同構成高階玻
纖布的實質進入障礙，亦使中國廠商產能雖快速擴張，短期內能實際轉化為高
階產品供給的比例仍有限。

供需失衡與料源綁定 
玻纖布供需失衡自 2026 年起由通用布延伸至高階布，主要瓶頸集中於織機、
良率與認證。玻纖布織機由豐田獨家供應，新機交期已排至 2031 年，且超薄
與特殊布須降速生產，使名目機台數無法等比轉化為實際產出；另一方面，高
階布認證週期普遍超過兩年，也使既有產能難以在短期內快速切換料號。 
E 布短缺主要來自龍頭廠商退出低階產能。日東紡、旭化成陸續停產 E-glass 
布後，供給收縮帶動各類 E 布報價自 2025 年底至 2026 年普遍上漲逾一倍
以上。惟中國新增產能自 2027 年陸續開出，加上消費性需求仍偏弱，研究部
預期 E 布供需將於 2027 年逐步緩解，報價漲幅趨緩。 
一代低介電布供需相對平衡，惟隨二代布需求放大，具備雙線能力的廠商傾向
將織機轉向二代布。二代低介電布則為目前供給最為吃緊的品項，主要對應 
M8 以上板材。除織機供給受限外，良率更是限制有效產出的關鍵，新進者量
產良率多不足五成，使名目產能與實際出貨量存在明顯落差，也支撐二代布報
價維持在一代布的約 2 至 3 倍。 
低膨脹布的供給缺口則可能延續更久。隨先進封裝尺寸放大與層數增加，而供
應仍高度集中於日東紡，新產能最快自 2H27 起陸續開出。石英布則面臨不同
瓶頸，問題不在供給，而在加工良率與導入進度；由於鑽孔與除膠渣難度較
高，部分原規劃採用石英布的設計轉回二代低介電布，反而延長二代布的產品
週期。 
供給吃緊亦同步改變材料認證與採購模式。過去玻纖布多由 CCL 廠與板廠選
料，現階段雲端業者與系統廠已逐步直接認證布廠並指定料源，部分客戶另要
求非中國產能，CCL 廠亦透過長約提前鎖定高階布供給。高階玻纖布的競爭因
而由單純產能擴張，轉向認證、良率與料源掌握能力的競爭。

高階玻纖布具備配方專利與長認
證週期雙重門檻，限制新進者有
效產能釋放，也使富喬憑藉紗布
垂直整合與既有認證基礎建立明
顯競爭優勢。

玻纖布供給受限於織機、良率與
認證門檻，高階產品短缺延續並
推動客戶直接綁定料源，產業競
爭 重 心 由 產 能 擴 張 轉 向 認 證 能
力、量產良率與供應穩定性。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 6

圖三、二代低介電布供需缺口

資料來源：統一投顧預估

產能配置與產品組合 
富喬目前共有約 920 台織布機，其中台灣 490 台、東莞 430 台。受限於織
布機交期，富喬 2027 年以前的主要成長動能，是將既有機台由 E 布、一代
低介電布持續轉向單位產值與毛利率更高的二代低介電布，透過產品組合升級
放大既有產能效益。 
台灣廠部分機台維持生產 E 布，供應台系 CCL 廠與低軌衛星應用，其餘產能
則持續轉向二代低介電布，低膨脹布亦預計自 2027 年起逐步導入。海外方
面，東莞現有 430 台於 2026 年仍以 E 布為主，自 1Q27 起採台灣供紗、當
地織布模式分批轉作二代低介電布。泰國一期則規劃新增 500 台織機，預計
自 2H27 起分批到位，主要生產低介電布並承接客戶對非中國產能的需求。 
富喬能快速進行跨廠區與跨產品配置，關鍵在於紗、布垂直整合。公司自有電
子級玻纖紗可隨布種升級同步調整紗的配方與規格，降低對外部紗源的依賴。
隨台灣產能持續高階化、東莞轉產二代布及泰國新產能加入，研究部預估二代
低介電布營收占比將由 2025 年的 7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 
51%，同期毛利率亦由 27.6% 升至 44.7% 與 54.8%。

富喬以既有機台轉產高毛利二代
低介電布，並透過東莞轉線與泰
國新產能擴充高階產品供給，帶
動二代布營收占比與整體毛利率
持續提升。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 7

圖四、富喬產品組合演變（營收占比）

資料來源：統一投顧預估

營運概況 
1. 2H26 營運將改寫新高 
富喬 7、8 月營收分別為 8.54 億元（+24.0% MoM、+68.1% YoY）與 9.12 億
元（+6.8% MoM、+79.2% YoY） ，連續改寫單月新高。隨新一代 AI 伺服器與 
1.6T 交換器平台陸續放量，二代低介電布需求同步攀升，加以 6 月起調漲的
報價自 3Q26 全季認列，研究部預估 3Q26 營收 27.66 億元（+29.8% QoQ、
+80.4% YoY） ，改寫單季歷史新高。毛利率升至 48.8%（+8.8ppt QoQ、
+21.9ppt YoY） ，除產品組合與報價效益外，二代布量產良率亦已逐步改善。
營業利益 10.39 億元（+89.8% QoQ、+301.5% YoY） ，營益率 37.6%；稅後淨
利 8.58 億元（+1.8% QoQ、+203.2% YoY），EPS 1.40 元，EPS 較 2Q26 小幅
下滑係因盈餘轉增資後股數增加。展望 4Q26，隨著二代布出貨比重持續提升
以及良率續揚貢獻，研究部預估營收 30.51 億元（+10.3% QoQ、+86.4% 
YoY） 、毛利率 49.9%、EPS 1.62 元。

2. 2027 年成長動能轉向產品組合升級，EPS 估 9.30 元 
2027 年富喬獲利成長動能將由價格上漲轉向產品組合升級與新增產能貢獻。
台灣廠持續將既有機台由一代轉向二代低介電布，東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣供
紗、當地織布模式，分批將 E 布產能轉作二代布；泰國一期則自 2H27 起逐
步投產，主要承接客戶對非中國高階產能的需求。隨三地產能同步往高階產品
配置，研究部預估二代低介電布營收占比將由 2026 年的 27% 提升至 2027 
年的 51%，成為獲利成長的主要來源。研究部預估 2027 年營收 149.82 億元
（+52.4% YoY） ，並隨泰國產能開出及二代布占比提升呈逐季成長；毛利率升
至 54.8%，營益率達 45.7%，帶動稅後淨利 57.18 億元（+81.4% YoY），EPS 
9.30 元。

2H26 受惠二代低介電布需求放
量 、 漲價效益與良率改善 ， 富喬營
收與毛利率同步攀升，研究部預
期 3Q26、4Q26 營運將連續改寫
新高。

2027 年富喬成長動能將由漲價
轉 向 產 品 組 合 升 級 與 新 產 能 貢
獻 ， 隨台灣 、 東莞及泰國產能加速
轉向二代低介電布，研究部預估
二代布營收占比升至 51%，帶動 
EPS 成長至 9.30 元。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 8

投資建議 
研究部初次給予富喬買進投資評等，主要考量：(1) 石英布受鑽孔損耗與加
工良率限制 ， 導入時程遞延 ， 終端客戶轉回採用二代低介電布搭配高階樹脂的
架構，延長二代布產品週期，帶動富喬二代低介電布營收占比由 2025 年的 
7%，提升至 2026 年的 27% 與 2027 年的 51%；(2) 玻纖布織布機由豐田獨
家供應，目前新機交期已排至 2031 年，意味 2027 年以前產業新增供給主
要仰賴既有機台重新配置 ， 富喬可在無須大幅增加資本支出的情況下 ， 持續將
既有產能由 E 布轉向一代及二代低介電布，研究部推算二代布毛利率較一代
布高逾 10 個百分點，產品組合優化將推升毛利率由 2025 年的 27.6%，提
升至 2026 年的 44.7% 與 2027 年的 54.8%；(3) 東莞廠自 1Q27 起採台灣
供紗、當地織布模式，分批將既有產能轉作二代低介電布，泰國一期則規劃  
500 台織機，自 2H27 起逐步開出，同樣以低介電布為主要產品，並承接客
戶對非中國產能的需求 。綜上所述 ， 研究部預估富喬 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 
分別為 5.13 元與 9.30 元，稅後純益分別年增 267.9% 與 81.4%。考量富
喬為日系龍頭以外少數具備紗、布垂直整合與自有配方 能力的供應商，以  
2027 年獲利為評價基礎，給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 185 元，初次給予買
進投資評等。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

## Document 047: 2330台積電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s new? EMC (2383.TW) shares fell 7.7% and TUC (6274.TWO) shares declined
6.7% on Sep 17, vs TAIEX +0.8%. Following the sell-off, investors have questioned
whether CPO could disrupt demand for high-end CCL. The concern is that
integrating optical engines alongside the ASIC on the package substrate shortens
high-speed electrical signal paths that would otherwise traverse the printed circuit

board (PCB), potentially reducing the need for ultra-low-Dk/Df, high-speed CCL.
Our thoughts:  This is not a new debate. We previously discussed the potential
implications of CPO for high-speed CCL demand here and here.  We believe this is
particularly relevant for scale-up architectures, where CPO adoption still faces
meaningful challenges related to cost, manufacturing yield, reliability, serviceability,
and thermal management. As such, we believe the risk of a broad-based reduction in

CCL requirements across AI systems before 2028 remains limited.  We would view
CPO-driven pullbacks in CCL stocks as potential opportunities to accumulate.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Co-packaged optics (CPO) is a long-term risk, not an imminent demand
disruption.
While we agree that broader CPO adoption represents a credible longer-term risk
to premium copper-clad laminate (CCL) content, we see limited risk to the
demand outlook over 2026–28.
We see limited near-term impact on CCL demand or our earnings estimates for

EMC and TUC, leaving our premium-CCL supply-tightness thesis and constructive
fundamental view intact.

## Document 071: 260921_daiwa_winway

Source PDF: all report pdf/260921_daiwa_winway.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-21
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on Winway’s business outlook.

What’s the impact: Marked revenue upside, but a more conservative 
gross margin outlook. We raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 9.3% to 
TWD5.0bn (+177.8% YoY) to reflect the stronger-than-expected execution 
of capacity expansion at Renwu Phase 1. Management guided for flat QoQ 
revenue in 4Q26, due to: 1) seasonality; 2) existing capacity being nearly 
fully utilised, with additional capacity scheduled to come online in 1H27; 
and 3) potential revenue recognition delays into 1H27 to accommodate 
customers’ schedules. On profitability, we lower our 3Q26E gross margin 
by 1.8pp, as we expect higher revenue contribution from probe cards and 
incremental depreciation from the new plant to dilute margins. Overall, for 
2026E and 2027E, we raise our revenue by 3.2% and 0.9%, while lowering 
our gross margin by 1.2pp and 1.7pp, respectively.

Probe card share concerns appear overdone. In response to concerns 
over probe card order losses, we believe the expanding TAM will attract 
competitors; the key differentiator is whether suppliers can scale capacity 
to meet customers’ demands. Winway does not expect its probe card share 
at its largest customer to decline and remains confident in maintaining its 
market-leading position. Its Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS) 
probe card supply agreement with Technoprobe (TPRO IM, Not Rated) 
remains in place, which should support its technology competitiveness.

Socket capacity expansion remains on track. Winway doubled its socket 
capacity YoY in 2026 and remains on track to reach monthly pogo-pin 
capacity of 14m units by 1H27, per management. We estimate monthly 
capacity to grow further to 16-18m units by end-2027. Management’s long-
term target is to raise its pogo-pin self-sufficiency ratio to 50-60%, which 
should support gross margin expansion through greater vertical integration.

What we recommend: We slightly raise revenue top-line but lower our 
2026-28E EPS by 1-5% to factor in conservative gross margin 
assumptions. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call, with 12M TP of TWD10,000, 
based on a PER of 50x, applied to our 2027E forward EPS. Our 2026-28E 
EPS CAGR of 100% makes our target PER undemanding, given that the 
resulting PEG ratio is 0.5x. Key downside risks: intensifying competition.

How we differ: Our revised 2026-27E EPS are now roughly in line with 
consensus, reflecting a more prudent gross margin. While market noise 
persists, we see no structural threat to Winway's leading position in sockets 
and probe cards.

21 September 2026

Capacity ramp drives revenue upside

➢ Revenue upside with more prudent gross margin assumptions 
➢ Probe card position intact; socket expansion on track 
➢ Reaffirming Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD10,000

Company profile 
Founded in Kaohsiung in 2001, WinWay has evolved from an optoelectronic test-fixture supplier into a 
leading logic test-interface provider. Its portfolio includes test sockets, MEMS probe cards and contact 
elements. Growth is driven by increasing AI/HPC testing complexity, GPU and ASIC design wins, 
expanding SLT adoption and next-generation HyperSocket solutions.

### Page 2

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Strategy and positioning: Management launched PhotonLink as an architecture-
agnostic integrated optics platform covering CPO, NPO, and chip-to-chip optical
connections. It spans the full optical chain: Generating the light, shaping and
guiding it, and detecting it at the far end. Management discussed that its
customers asked it to supply complete assemblies rather than individual
components as stitching together lasers, fiber and passive optics is difficult. It

argued that no other company holds the full portfolio in-house and that Coherent
can manufacture at datacenter scale. Management expects a mix of adoption
paths, with some customers going straight to CPO and others starting with NPO
and moving to CPO later.
Figure 1. COHR's Portfolio of Optical AI Networking Products
Source: COHR at ECOC
Customers and content: In a full-solution example (combining the ELS, the
assembly to the switch or XPU, and the assembly out to the faceplate),

management said Coherent content could reach up to $15k of content per
switch/processor chip needing 100T of I/O capacity at 200G optical lane. Coherent
counts more than 10 customer engagements each in CPO and NPO and more than
5 in chip-to-chip, with activity going up sharply over the past six months. For CPO,
management said it has an anchor customer with an LTA secured, which it
identified as NVDA. It also has an anchor customer and LTA for NPO, which it did

not name. Separately, management cited two LTAs for ultra-high-power CW lasers
with leading hyperscale or AI datacenter customers, also unnamed.

### Page 3

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Figure 2. PhontonLink Platform
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Timing: CPO for scale-out, tied to the NVIDIA agreement, begins ramping in the
December quarter (calendar Q4 2026). CPO for scale-up follows in the second half
of calendar 2027. NPO ramps in the second half of next calendar year, mainly for
scale-up. Chip-to-chip is further out, around 2029-2030.
Light generation: The ultra-high-power CW laser sits in an external laser source

that holds up to eight lasers, each feeding roughly four links. It starts ramping this
quarter, with significant volume next year, on the 6-inch InP platform in Sherman,
Texas. For NPO, a high-power buried-heterostructure CW laser is already shipping
in millions of units per month for pluggables. VCSEL arrays come in a conventional
version for ribbon fiber and a high-density 2D version that can be flip-chipped
directly to drivers and TIAs with backside lenses. Photodetectors run to 200G

today, with 400G in development.
Figure 3. UHP CW Laser Leader
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026

### Page 4

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Fab footprint: Sherman runs 6-inch InP and GaAs, Sweden runs 6-inch InP . Zurich
runs 6-inch GaAs and is adding 6-inch InP for high-power lasers, while Fremont
runs a 3-inch line. GaAs capacity has grown fourfold since the 6-inch move about
six years ago, with capacity for over one billion VCSEL arrays per year. InP capacity
doubled last year, a quarter ahead of schedule, and management plans to double it

again or more.
Figure 4. COHR Capacity Expansion
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Shape, guide, and receive: Coherent grows garnet in the United States and says it
supplies a significant fraction of the industry's garnet and isolators, with
customers signing LTAs for capacity. The ELS is built on the transceiver line and is
sampling now. The rest of the portfolio covers specialty fiber (polarization-
maintaining fiber production-ready, mode-matched fiber introduced at ECOC,

multi-core fiber), micro-lens arrays, V-grooves, fiber array units, a detachable
connector, circulators, WDM multiplexers, and fiber shuffles.
Silicon photonics and engines: Management discussed Coherent having an in-
house silicon photonics design team, uses foundries for wafers, and does assembly
and test in-house. It ships 200G-per-lane silicon photonics in transceivers today
and showed a 400G link at OFC with a post-deadline paper. It has a 6.4T silicon

photonics NPO module (32 lanes of 200G) and a VCSEL-based NPO with 2D
arrays, which management says achieves about 1.2 pJ/bit including the laser.
Architecture trade-off: Management said NPO is easier to deploy and service but
costs power and signal integrity versus CPO, and that a common NPO socket can
take silicon photonics or VCSELs. It positioned Coherent as an honest advisor
because it can compare all the options. Chip-to-chip is a growing topic, with WDM

and circulators important there.

### Page 5

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
5
Figure 5. COHR CPO Offerings
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Figure 6. COHR NPO Offerings
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Key takeaways from Citi discussion with management:
What is Coherent's competitive advantage in 3.2T, and will it participate?
Management said it has already demonstrated the building blocks for the 3.2T
generation, including 400G-per-lane EMLs and 400G silicon photonics, and
expects to participate just as it enables 1.6T today. Management showed these

demonstrations as early as two years ago.
What is the gross margin path, and what offsets mix pressure? Management
mentioned that the 42%+ target was deliberately open-ended and that it expects
to exceed it. It cautioned against expecting a step change over the next two to
three quarters. Fast transceiver growth is a mix headwind, offset by overhead
absorption, yield gains and factory consolidation. Management expects the 800G-
to-1.6T transition to be margin-accretive, with the cutover forecast around 2028.

### Page 6

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
6
OCS, shipping for two to three quarters, is the first of the new platforms to
generate revenue. OCS, CPO and multi-rail will contribute more to margins as mix
builds.
What are the challenges and economics of the 6-inch InP transition?
Management said it announced 6-inch capability in March 2024 after years of
work with substrate vendors that began before the AI boom. Output is expected to
be roughly 50/50 between 3-inch and 6-inch by the end of the quarter, with all

incremental capacity on 6-inch, reaching about 75% by the end of 2027. A 6-inch
die costs about half as much per yielded die, and each line delivers about four
times the capacity. Yields are better on modern tooling. Management cited a three-
to-four-year lead over competitors and secured substrate supply from five
suppliers across three continents. It also credited its 6-inch GaAs VCSEL line to a
large consumer-electronics customer that demanded the larger wafer size.

Will Coherent keep external InP suppliers? COHR only sources EMLs externally
and that it will keep some external supply for continuity on a critical component.
How does Coherent differ from Chinese laser suppliers? Management argued
that module assembly is easier to replicate but high-speed lasers are not. It said
there are no high-speed EMLs or broadband tunable lasers from China, and that
Chinese CW lasers top out around 70-100 mW. It sees only one InP laser

competitor worth naming and expects new Chinese module entrants to take share
from existing Chinese suppliers rather than from Coherent.
Does NPO vs CPO matter for content, and can low-power lasers serve NPO?
Management said it does not matter much, because the components and dollar
content are similar. Designs are custom and take 18 to 24 months to reach volume,
and management reiterated scale-up production revenue in the second half of next
year. Management rejected the view that NPO can use low-power lasers, arguing

that coupling several weak lasers loses light and defeats the power-efficiency
purpose.
What is multi-rail, and how is it progressing? Multi-rail addresses scale-across
links between data centers. Management said it delivers four times the capacity in
the same footprint at roughly 30% lower power per lane, enabled by a multi-chip
uncooled pump that it says has no comparable competitor. Small shipments are
underway and production shipments begin in the first half of next calendar year. It

sells at the component, module, line-card and system level, including white-label
to OEMs.

### Page 7

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Coherent Corp
Company description
COHR is a vertically integrated manufacturing company that develops
engineered materials and opto-electronic components for industrial
materials processing, communications, aerospace and defense, consumer
electronics, semiconductor capital equipment, life sciences and automotive
end markets. COHR produces a wide variety of application-specific photonic
and electronic materials and components, and deploys them in various forms,

including integration with advanced software. COHR's customer base
includes original equipment manufacturers, laser end users, system
integrators of high-power lasers, manufacturers of equipment and devices for
industrial, optical communications, consumer electronics, security and
monitoring applications, U.S. government prime contractors, and various U.S.
government agencies. COHR is a headquartered in Pennsylvania.
Investment strategy
We are Buy-rated on COHR. We believe that the stock's multiple will benefit

from the AI appeal while its fundamentals should expand on new
management's strong execution and focus on profitability.
Valuation
We value COHR at $420 or 30x forward P/E applied to FY2028 EPS of ~$16
discounted back. Our COHR valuation is nevertheless at a 10% discount to
the average forward P/E of AI optics peers to reflect a lower AI sales mix and
3-year EPS CAGR vs peers such as LITE.
Risks
Downside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) increased

competition on AI and SiC leads to limited market share growth and limited
revenue upside, 2) inability to achieve synergies and margin targets
established post acquisition of Finisar, and 3) prolonged economic recession
leads to slower 5G build-out and lower consumer demand driving revenue of
COHR down.
Upside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) gaining share in
AI and SiC market through new product offerings and first to market status

on larger wafer sizes, 2) quicker and stronger adoption of AI workloads,
electric and autonomous vehicles, and 3) quicker return of margins to pre-
Finisar acquisition levels.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

ab 23 September 2026
Global Research
Key Call: MediaTek Inc.
Multiple catalysts ahead on TPU; quantifying
upside in new ASIC projects
Stronger TPU upside in 2028-29E
We believe the market has not fully priced in MediaTek's strategic value and opportunity
in Google TPU, and view several catalysts ahead. We keep TPU sales at US$18bn in
2027E, but raise 2028/29E to US$43.5bn/52.5bn from 35.0/40.0bn previously, as we
factor in longer product cycle of v8t in 2028E and stronger growth of v9 in 2029E. See

our TPU financial analysis in Figure 1U B S  f o r e c a s t s  f o r   M e d ia T e k ' s  T P U  b u s in e s s . Catalysts in next 6-9 months include 1) v9 tape out
delivery by Nov 2026E, 2) improving visibility and execution of Intel EMIB-T, 3) Google
further raising v9 volume forecast in H127 assuming constructive feedback from v9
system verification, 4) MediaTek upgrading TPU guidance during their earnings
conference in Oct, Jan/Feb.
MediaTek potentially adding CoWoS on top of EMIB-T to ramp more v9 in 2028-

29
We are increasingly more optimistic about MediaTek's TPU v9 volume potential in 2028-
29. Overall execution of Google and MediaTek appears smoother for v9 compared with
v8t, and the tape out is so far on track for delivery by Nov 2026. On EMIB-T, our deep
dive suggests better than feared execution for substrate manufacturing at Ibiden, with
increasing support from Unimicron, Shinko, and more suppliers. EMIB-T supply should
be sufficient to drive >2m v9 volume in 2028. However, EMIB-T substrate could be tight

if v9 volume grows to 4m or more in 2029. We believe that, likely due to supply
consideration, MediaTek could be engaging with TSMC to add a CoWoS solution for v9.
It's still early stage, but could reflect the meaningful upside potential for v9 into 2029.
Upside risk from new ASIC projects
MediaTek has been proactively exploring new projects to further scale ASICs sales. There
are two projects with higher possibility, although not yet finalized. 1) SpaceX's Dojo 3

based on TSMC N2 and SoW (System on Wafer) packaging. MediaTek could be
collaborating with Nvidia to offer NVLink Fusion to secure this project. 2) Tesla's AI6 for
ADAS and robotics, which could be first produced at Intel fab, followed by Terafab. Tesla
is already deploying AI6 at TSMC and Samsung Foundry, but the goal could be to move
more volume to Terafab if the ramp is successful. Our initial view is both projects
combined could drive up to US$10-15bn ASIC revenue opportunity for MediaTek. We

expect both projects to be finalized soon in coming months given the goal to enter mass
production by 2029.
Valuation: reiterate Key Call Buy; raising price target to NT$7,300
We fine tune 2027E EPS on softer smartphone outlook but raise 2028/29E EPS by
21/28%. Our 2028 EPS estimate is 44% above consensus. Raise price target to NT
$7,300 from NT$6,500 based on unchanged 27x 2027-28E PE. Reiterate Key Call Buy.

Company Description
MediaTek is a leading integrated circuit (IC) design company in Taiwan, focusing on solutions
from the edge to the cloud, applications for its product offerings range from smartphones,
smart homes and AI PCs to high-performance computing, automotive, and AI data centers.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for MediaTek is based on a target PE methodology.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights. In
addition, MediaTek is exposed to potential price competition in handset SoC, TV SoC, etc.

Downside risks include slower-than-expected market share gains and a weaker-than-
expected demand recovery.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 081: 260923_daiwa_Topoint Technology

Source PDF: all report pdf/260923_daiwa_Topoint Technology.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Topoint was among the world’s top 3 PCB drill-bit suppliers 
by revenue and sales volume in 2025. It supplies drill bits and router bits to 
PCB manufacturers and expanded into PCB drilling services in 2010. In 
2Q26, drill bits accounted for 68% of revenue, while drilling services 
accounted for 32%. It operates drill-bit manufacturing facilities and drilling-
service sites in Taiwan, China and Thailand.

Highlights: AI PCB upgrades are driving drill-bit market growth. AI 
PCBs increasingly feature 30+ layers, M8-grade CCL or higher-grade 
materials, and denser routing, driving demand for PCB drill bits in 3 ways: 
1) more interconnects increase the number of holes to be drilled; 2) thicker, 
harder boards accelerate bit wear and raise consumption; and 3) more 
demanding drilling requirements support greater adoption of high-end bits. 
Frost & Sullivan forecasts the global PCB drill-bit market to grow at a 9.6% 
CAGR in 2026-30, outpacing the global PCB market.

Customer investment underscores tight drill-bit supply. With drill-bit 
capacity running at near-full utilisation, management expects monthly drill-
bit capacity to rise from 32m units at end-2025 to 45-50m at end-2026, 70-
75m at end-2027 and 90-100m at end-2028. Notably, key customers 
Unimicron (3037 TT, TWD1,160, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng), GCE 
(2368 TT, TWD1,140, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng) and Zhen Ding 
(4958 TT, not rated) have participated in Topoint’s private convertible-bond 
offering, providing funding for its expansion.

Coated drill bits and high-aspect-ratio drilling driving spec upgrades. 
Tungsten is a key raw material for PCB drill bits, with Topoint sourcing it 
based on plant location. Topoint adjusts prices to reflect changes in costs 
and raised prices by 20% in 1H26. Alongside price hikes, a richer product 
mix lifted 2Q26 gross margin to a record high, up 5.2pp QoQ and 12.3pp 
YoY. As PCBs become harder, demand is shifting towards coated drill bits. 
Coatings such as diamond-like carbon improve wear resistance and bit life, 
while helping to maintain hole-wall quality and drilling yields. High-end 
coated products accounted for 56% of the drill-bit shipment mix in 1H26, up 
from 48% in 2025. Thicker boards and smaller hole diameters are also 
driving a shift towards micro drill bits with high aspect ratios, supporting 
ASPs and margins. Looking ahead, next-gen CCL materials present further 
drilling challenges: PTFE can adhere to the bit during machining, while 
quartz fibre accelerates wear. Per Topoint, these spec upgrades will sustain 
product-mix improvement and support higher margins over the long term.

Valuation: Topoint is currently trading at 17.7/13.8x 2027/28E PER based 
on Bloomberg consensus (vs. its average 1-year forward PER of 27x).

23 September 2026

A key supplier riding the drill-bit upcycle

➢ Top-3 global drill-bit supplier expanding capacity with customer backing 
➢ Ongoing high-end drill-bit upgrades to sustain margin expansion 
➢ Currently trading at 18x/14x 2027/28E PER based on consensus

Asus (2357.TW): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix 
upgrade; Aug beat; Neutral
27 September 2026 | 10:33AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep, leading 
3Q26 revenues to grow at 8% QoQ, while we expect sequential revenues decline in 
4Q26 at 7%, considering the PC consumption pull in under the high memory costs 
along with AI servers model transition. We remain positive on the company’s 
expansion toward AI servers and expect the upcoming new models carrying higher 
ASP to further drive its growth in late 2026 / early 2027. We continue to expect 
double digit revenues YoY in 3Q26 / 4Q26E at 39% / 27% YoY driven by PC product 
mix upgrade and AI server racks ramp up. The company retains strong commitments 
on capacity expansion and diversification, with issuance of US$0.5bn of convertible 
bonds and US$1bn of exchangeable bonds, supporting the expansion. Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Earnings revision: We factor in ASUS’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 4% / 7% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues. 2026-28E revenues are raised 
by 4% / 4% / 4%, mainly on robust AI server growth momentum, and PC product mix 
upgrade along with rising selling price under the high memory costs.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,115. Our target 
price is based on 14.8x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

### Page 2

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Gold Circuit Electronics
Valuation
Our TP of NT$1,630 for GCE is based on 25x our 2027E EPS estimate, which is higher than 22x or +2std level of its 1-year-
forward PE over the past three years. We believe the 25x multiple is justified by GCE's high AI server/networking sales
exposure, market share gain in existing ASIC customer and new AI ASIC customer wins from 4Q26, which drives its AI ASIC

sales growth and margin profile. Recall that TSMC expects close to a 50%+ CAGR in the next five years for its AI-related
demand, which is stronger than any other applications. Thus, we believe investors would be willing to pay for high-growth AI
names (especially ASIC) with high PE multiples. Our target price is equivalent to 44x/25x our 2026/27E EPS estimates and
16.5x/9.9x our 2026/27E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected server

recovery; 2) slower ramp-up pace of new server platform; 3) less-than-expected AI demand; 4) more PCB peers added into AI
supply chain; and 5) stricter practice of China+1 by customers due to geopolitical concerns.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Short-Term View on Mediatek (2454.TW)

Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Taiwan ODM/Brands: 3-month preview: Aug +9% MoM on avg; new AI 
server racks and smartphone models to drive growth in coming months
30 September 2026 | 1:20AM HKT
We preview 3-month revenues for 10 companies under coverage in the AI server/PC 
supply chain, and update our estimates for Wistron and Inventec. Across the supply 
chain, we are positive on: (1) Apple’s supply chain on the back of a ramp up of new 
models with form factor changes (i.e. foldable phones in 2026, and the iPhone 20 in 
2027) to support end demand; (2) AI server components to ride on specification 
upgrades; and (3) ASIC AI servers’ rising penetration rate. Buy: Hon Hai, Wiwynn, 
Wistron, AVC; Neutral: Gigabyte, Quanta, Inventec, Compal, ASUS; Sell: Pegatron.  
On a MoM basis, we expect the average revenue growth of the 10 companies to 
come in at +7%/ +2%/ +4% in Sep/ Oct / Nov 2026 (Exhibit 1). We expect 
sequential revenue growth in coming months, driven by the next-generation 
rack-level AI server and new flagship smartphone models shipments ramp up. 
On a YoY basis, we expect the average revenue growth of the 10 companies to 
come in at +49%/ +57%/ +52% in Sep/ Oct / Nov 2026 (Exhibit 2), driven by the 
continuous ramp-up of AI servers, along with a PC product mix upgrade, new 
smartphone models, and high memory costs driving up the selling price.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 3Q / 4Q26E; 3-month 
revenues preview

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +14% / +23% QoQ, driven by (1) AI data 
center: The next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp up, with higher ASP 
and early delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first one to deliver the latest model, 
around 2-3 months ahead of peers, driving its Sep and onward revenue growth. 
Followed by GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks would fuel 
the growth in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27, and (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlight in September (report link), we 
reduce global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m to 1.1bn / 1.2bn/ 1.2bn 
in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we remain 
positive on global leading brands to outperform the market given the strong brand 
awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger consumption power. We 
remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium models to continue gain 
market share, positive to Hon Hai. Maintain Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect company’s 3Q26E revenues to be +40% YoY/ 
+14% QoQ to NT$2.9 trillion. Both Jul and Aug delivered strong revenues YoY growth 
(+54%/ 52% YoY), and we expect to see both YoY and MoM positive growth in Sep to 
Dec, driven by new AI server racks, CPO switch, and latest smartphone models and 
foldable phones. We keep 2026E - 2028E earnings estimates largely unchanged.

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): New AI server rack ramp up to support 2H26 growth; PC slowdown; 
Neutral

With a high base in 1H26, driven by PC consumption pull-in and AI server rack 
expansion, we expect flat QoQ in 3Q26 / 4Q26 at 2% / 2%, reflecting slowdown in PC 
shipment and AI server racks facing model transitions. The company’s PC shipment 
declined by 30% / 37% YoY in Jul / Aug, reflecting the impact of PC consumption pull-in 
in 1H26 and slow PC shipment under high memory costs. We expect revenues to 
sequentially increase in Oct to Dec and further accelerate to 54% MoM in Dec, reflecting 
the ramp up of next generation AI server racks, which carry higher ASP . With strong AI 
spending driving not only AI servers (GPU and ASIC) but also general servers, along with 
customer requirement on capacity diversification, we estimate Quanta’s capex to stay at 
a high level of NT$44bn / NT$32bn in 2026 / 27E (vs. NT$15bn in 2025). In Sep, Quanta 
announced factory rental in USA at 199k square feet (US$368k per month for 126

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling rising adoption along with new AI servers across GPU and ASIC to 
drive sequential monthly revenues growth; Buy

3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue sequential 
growth through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +18% / +27% QoQ, 
driven by (1) new AI server racks ramp up, driving overall liquid cooling penetration rate. 
As we highlighted in June (report link), we expect AI server rack (NVL72-equivalent) 
shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 55k/ 105k/ 163k in 2026-28E, increasing by 
16% / 20% in 2027 / 28E, reflecting our positive view on AI server racks, (2) its major 
ASIC AI server customer’s new model ramp up, driving its air-cooling and liquid-cooling 
components, along with a rising liquid cooling penetration rate, (3) its customer base 
expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further drive its liquid cooling components in 
4Q26, and (4) increasing adoption rate of ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit the 
lightweight design and major customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s net 
income to grow at a +39% CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a 
growing liquid cooling penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total 
solutions (air cooling, liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,513 on a target P/E multiple of 30.3x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition;

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion; Aug strong beat; Buy

Wiwynn delivered strong Aug revenues, which were 27% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general servers demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI servers ramping up. We expect sequential revenues growth to continue 
through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +46% / +22% QoQ, driven 
by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. Maintain Buy with TP 
of NT$3,777 (21.5x 2027E P/E).

Ongoing capacity expansion: As we highlighted in Aug (report link), Wiwynn 
announced a 2H26 capex budget of US$942m, implying 2026 capex could be up to 
NT$40bn (US$1.3bn). On Sep 16, the company announced equipment procurement of 
US$16m (NT$512m), reflecting the strong AI demand and the company’s commitments 
on capacity expansion. We estimate the company’s capex at NT$44bn in 2026E, and to 
stay at a similar high level through 2028E, supporting its production sites diversification 
across the US, Mexico, and Malaysia, AI servers expansion from ASIC to GPU-powered, 
and products expansion across different ASICs and GPUs, along with further customers 
penetration.

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): Aug strong beat on rack-level AI server; Buy with TP raised to NT$295

Wistron’s Aug revenue was up 166% YoY to NT$460bn, or 48% higher than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, offsetting the impact of 
AI server model transition and sluggish PC end demand. Aug PC shipment was down 
13% MoM to 1.4m units, mainly on the 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising memory 
cost. We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up across GPU, CPU, and LPU. The 
company plans to invest US$209m to expand its AI server capacity in US and Vietnam 
(link), which would further support its revenue growth in coming years, in our view. 
Maintain Buy.

income by 2% / 3% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 
11% / 7% / 6%, mainly on higher rack-level AI server shipments and ASP , considering the 
strong end demand and rising memory cost. Our 2026-28E GMs are revised down 
mainly on higher contribution from rack-level AI server, which usually carries lower GM 
than other product lines. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are largely unchanged.

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix upgrade

3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, mainly 
driven by AI server racks ramp up with higher ASP and major smartphone brand 
customer’s new model launches (report link). Overall, we project Pegatron’s 3Q/4Q26E 
revenues to grow +18%/+25% YoY, driven by: (1) new model launches from a major 
smartphone customer supporting September and 4Q26 revenue growth; (2) a 
continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments, across rack-level and baseboard-based, 
riding on the growing demand in Neocloud and enterprises, (3) broadband, IoT, and 
game console entering peak season. We are positive on Pegatron’s expansion from 
consumer electronics to AI servers and automotive electronics, while the high memory 
costs could cap PC and smartphone market growth. Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Aug revenue was up 34% YoY / 3% MoM to NT$88bn, or 12% lower than our 
estimate. This reflects that high memory costs continue to cap PC and smartphone 
growth, despite a strong ramp-up in AI servers. Aug PC shipments was down 49% YoY to 
0.40-0.45m, reflecting the impact of consumption pull in under the high memory costs. 
Its major smartphone brand customer launched new models in Sep, supporting 
Pegatron’s growth in Sep and 4Q26; prior to September, the company suffered from 
model transition (i.e. declining demand for older models while new models were in a 
slow shipment ramp-up phase).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

- 1 - 
個股報告 
嘉晶(3016) 
8 吋 Si Epi 滿載支撐獲利，Ge/Si 擴產加速 
投資評等 目標價 
區間操作 $152 
前次投資建議：2026.04.30 
「區間操作」 ，目標價: $92

報告日期：2026.09.23 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 嘉晶 26Q2 毛利率 23.5%優於預期，8吋 Si Epi 維持滿載，Ge/Si需求能見度延伸至 2028 年，
上修 2026/27F EPS 至 2.56/3.69，目標價 152 元(6.5x2027(F)PBR)，潛在上漲空間 14.7%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/09/22 收盤價： 132.50 ➢ 26Q2 營收 13.09 億元，季增 23.6%、年增 37.1%，毛
利率 23.5%、營益率16.9%，稅後淨利1.88 億元、EPS 
0.65 元。營收僅略高於研究部原估12.92 億元，毛利率
較原估 18.4%高出 5.1ppt，主要反映8吋產品占比提高
及稼動率改善，因此上修獲利。 
➢ 26H1 8 吋 Si Epi 產能滿載，8吋占 Si Epi 營收比重由
去年同期 59%提升至 71%，MOSFET 與 PMIC 晶圓產
出年增 50%。既有產能仍無法滿足客戶需求，2026 年
新增設備預計自 2027 年陸續開出。 
➢ Compound Epi 營運改善，26H1 GaN/SiC營收分別年
增 20%/28%；6 吋 GaN 已轉虧為盈且產能滿載，SiC
則受惠日本 IDM 客戶自 26Q2 開始放量，稼動率提高
後虧損明顯收斂。 Ge/Si PD Epi 26Q2 營收季增
113%，26H2 營收預估較 26H1 成長 2.5 倍，2027 年
產能預計增至目前 3.5～4倍，訂單能見度延伸至2028
年；惟 26H1 營收占比僅約 1%，研究部推估 2027 年
占比仍約 4%～5%，短期 EPS貢獻有限。 
➢ 研究部上修 2026 年營收至 53.3 億元、毛利率 23.1%、
EPS 2.56元；2027 年營收 68.7 億元、毛利率25.1%、
EPS 3.69元 。 考量8吋 Si Epi維持滿載 、Compound Epi
獲利改善及 Ge/Si後續成長，給予 6.5x 27F PBR，目
標價 152 元。6.5x 已位於公司過去約 2–8x PBR 區間
偏高位置，反映目前獲利改善及成長能見度提升，評價
進一步上修空間有限；潛在上漲空間 14.7%，維持「區
間操作」評等。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析 
嘉晶為國內少數同時跨足矽磊晶、化合物半導體與矽光子之專業磊晶代工廠 
嘉晶（3016）成立於 1996 年，總部位於新竹科學園區，為國內專業半導體
磊晶 （Epitaxy） 代工廠，主要股東為漢民科技集團。公司早期以矽磊晶 （Silicon 
Epi）代工起家，產品應用於電源管理 IC（PMIC）、MOSFET 等功率半導
體領域；中期切入化合物半導體磊晶，建置氮化鎵（GaN）與碳化矽（SiC）
產線，跨入射頻與功率元件市場，下游應用涵蓋資料中心電源、電動車、再
生能源、人形機器人與航太科技；近期則進一步切入矽光子（ Silicon 
Photonics）用 Ge on Si（鍺在矽上）光偵測器（PD）磊晶代工，為國內少
數同時具備三大磊晶平台量產能力之專業業者。 
公司於台灣設有 4 吋、5 吋、6 吋、8 吋多尺寸磊晶產線，磊晶製程涵蓋  
Silicon、GaN、SiC、Ge on Si 等多種材料系統，具備磊晶配方設計、設備
運轉、品質與良率管控之完整 Know-How。客戶結構上，下游涵蓋 Fabless 
設計業者與 IDM 廠，訂單來源以台灣為主，日本與其他地區次之。

圖一、嘉晶(3016) 2026H1產品別營收比重

資料來源：嘉晶，兆豐投顧彙整

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2 毛利率優於預期，上修 2026年獲利 
嘉晶 26Q2 營收 13.09 億元，季增 23.6%、年增 37.1%，毛利率 23.5%，季
增 5.7ppt，營益率 16.9%，稅後淨利 1.88 億元、EPS 0.65 元；26H1 營收
23.68億元 、 年增27.9%， 毛利率20.9%，EPS 1.00元 ， 較去年同期EPS -0.14
元明顯改善。 
26Q2營收與研究部原估 12.92億元差異有限 ， 惟毛利率23.5%較原估 18.4%
高出 5.1ppt，獲利優於預期主要來自產品組合及稼動率改善。26H1 持續將
小尺寸產能轉往 8 吋，8 吋產品占比提高，加上 8 吋產線維持滿載，帶動固
定成本吸收及整體獲利改善。26Q2 毛利率尚未反映漲價效益，價格調整自
26Q3 才開始，26H2 將陸續反映效益。  
研究部將 2026 年營收由前次 51.0 億元上修至 53.3 億元，年增 37.0%；毛
利率由 18.1%上修至 23.1%，稅後淨利 7.6億元、EPS由 1.70 元上修至 2.56
元。預估 26Q3/26Q4 營收 14.59/15.06 億元，季增 11.5%/3.2%，毛利率
24.6%/24.9%，EPS均為 0.8元。

8吋 Si Epi維持滿載，2027年新增產能陸續開出 
Si Epi 仍為嘉晶主要營收與獲利來源，應用涵蓋 PMIC、Power MOSFET、
IGBT、Driver 及 Protection IC 等功率元件。AI 伺服器功率密度提升，帶動
PMIC與 Power MOSFET 需求增加，26H1 8吋 Si Epi產能滿載，營收占 Si 
Epi比重由去年同期 59%提升至 71%，MOSFET與 PMIC晶圓產出年增50%。 
國內部分晶圓廠原先具備自有 Epi產能，但AI 相關 PMIC需求增加後，既有
產能不足，帶動委外需求增加。26H2 Si Epi 營收預估年增約 42%，8 吋既
有產能滿載後仍無法滿足客戶需求，目前客戶持續要求擴產，2027 年新增產
能開出後仍為主要營收成長來源。 
AI伺服器功率需求亦帶動不同材料平台成長，其中Si Epi主要對應 PMIC 與
MOSFET；800V高壓直流架構則增加 GaN及 SiC等化合物半導體的應用機
會 。 三項產品雖均受惠AI電源需求 ， 但目前對嘉晶獲利的貢獻仍以8吋 Si Epi
為主。

26Q3 開始調價，26H2營收與獲利優於 26H1 
原物料及相關成本上升，自 26Q3 開始針對不同產品與客戶調整價格。由於
價格調整自 26Q3 才開始 ，26Q2 毛利率由 17.8%提升至 23.5%主要仍反映 8
吋占比及產能利用率改善，而非 ASP上升。26H2 Si Epi 需求維持高檔，加
上價格調整開始反映，研究部預估26Q3/26Q4 毛利率分別為 24.6%/24.9%，
高於 26Q2 的 23.5%。26H2 營收 29.65 億元，較 26H1 成長 25.2%，2026
全年營收 53.3 億元、年增 37.0%，符合公司全年營收年增>35%的展望。 
2027 年毛利率預估進一步提升至 25.1%，主要反映產品組合持續改善、
Compound Epi 獲利回升，以及 Ge/Si較高毛利產品占比增加；另一方面，
2026 年新增設備的折舊將自 2027～28年陸續認列，部分抵銷產品組合改善
效益，因此並未假設 Si Epi毛利率持續大幅上升。

圖二、嘉晶(3016)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

Compound Epi營運改善，GaN轉盈、SiC虧損收斂 
26H1 Compound Epi 營運明顯改善，GaN營收年增 20%、SiC年增 28%。
GaN 自 25Q3 至 26Q2 連續四季成長，6 吋產能目前已滿載並由虧轉盈，8
吋則開始小量生產；現有 6 吋需求高於可供產能，客戶有對公司提出擴產需
求。GaN 後續擴產狀況須配合客戶產品平台及下游 Foundry 進度。目前 6
吋訂單充足，但長期產業逐步往 8吋發展，因此新增產能將依客戶與Foundry
合作成熟度調整。SiC 方面，日本 IDM客戶自 26Q2 開始放量，帶動產能利
用率回升，原先投資虧損明顯縮小。中國SiC市場仍面臨較激烈的價格競爭，
目前嘉晶業務以日本及其他非中國市場為主。

26H2 Compound Epi 營收預估年增 86%，改善幅度高於 Si Epi。惟現階段
Compound Epi營收規模仍低於 Si Epi，2026～27年對獲利的主要貢獻在於
GaN由虧轉盈及 SiC虧損持續收斂，而非取代 Si Epi成為主要獲利來源。

Compound Epi 營運改善，GaN轉盈、SiC虧損收斂 
Ge/Si PD Epi主要應用於高速光通訊接收端 Photodiode，目前對應800G 及
1.6T 可插拔式光模組。26Q2 營收季增 113%，26H2 營收預估較 26H1成長
約 2.5 倍。。需求端能見度已延伸至 2028 年，目前產能只能滿足既有客戶
不到 50%的需求。2027 年產能預計增至目前 3.5～4倍，但依現有訂單需求
仍不足，後續仍有追加設備空間；主要擴產限制來自設備交期。

Ge/Si成長率雖高，但短期對整體EPS貢獻有限。26H1營收占比僅約 1%，
依 26H2 成長幅度及 2027 年 3.5～4 倍成長假設推估，2027 年營收約 3 億

元，占研究部 2027F 營收 68.7 億元約 4%～5%。即使 Ge/Si 毛利率明顯高
於一般 Epi， 基期仍小，2026～27年公司獲利仍主要取決於 8吋 Si Epi營收、
稼動率及毛利率。

擴產效益自 2027年陸續反映，獲利延續成長 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 53.3 億元、年增 37.0%，毛利率 23.1%、營益率
16.7%，稅後淨利 7.60 億元、EPS 2.56 元；2027 年營收 68.7 億元、年增
28.8%，毛利率 25.1%、營益率 19.1%，稅後淨利 10.95 億元、EPS 3.69
元。 
2026 年獲利上修主要反映 26Q2 毛利率優於預期、26H2 8 吋 Si Epi 維持高
稼動率及 26Q3 開始調價；2027 年則受惠 8 吋新增產能陸續開出、GaN 獲
利增加與 SiC 虧損收斂，以及 Ge/Si營收占比提升。 
估值延續採用 PBR評價。嘉晶過去 PBR約落在 2～8x，考量 8吋 Si Epi 供
需改善、Compound Epi 營運轉佳及 Ge/Si中長期成長動能，給予 6.5x 27F 
PBR。6.5x 位於歷史區間偏高位置，已反映部分基本面改善與 2027 年成長
預期，後續評價倍數上修空間相對有限。上調目標價至 152 元(6.5x 27 (F) 
PBR)，潛在上漲空間 14.7%，維持「區間操作」評等。

欣銓 (3264 TT)  Buy

晶圓大廠新訂單逐步向上，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：欣銓 2026 年資本支出轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策
略客戶已確定之 ASIC 合作案。欣銓 1H26 在取得不動產、廠房
及設備的現金流出達新台幣 70.07 億元，較 1H25 的 18.65 億元
大幅增加約 51.4 億元。預期 2H26 則隨龍潭廠機台陸續交付並
完成數據驗證後，進入產能爬坡期，營收逐步向上。在排除匯
率波動前提下 ， 獲利亦預計維持逐季成長的趨勢 。 預估欣銓2026
年稅後 EPS 9.94 元。龍潭廠新專案是 2026~2027 年最重要的增
長引擎 。 公司與策略夥伴(晶圓廠 )合作的 ASIC 測試專案 07/2026
開始明顯增加，而旗下全智湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽光
子相關新產品的量產階段，都將帶動公司中長期營收獲利向上，
因此建議欣銓 Buy ，目標價 322 元 (2027 年 PER24X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.36 元： AI 相關需求佳和通訊相關訂單維持強
勁。欣銓 2Q26 營收為 45.34 億元， QoQ+13.81% 。營收依應用
別分析，通訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 33.4% ；車用 /安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 18.2% ；射頻晶片 (RF IC) 佔 12.2% ；微處理器
(General MCU) 佔 8.5% ； Storage 佔 10.8% ； PC/Consumers 佔
9.9% ；Memory 佔 5.4% ；其他 1.6% 。毛利率的部份， 2Q26 毛利
率為 40.17% ，增加 1.81 個百分點。 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.36 元。  
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.73 元：龍潭新廠歷經 1H26 客戶驗證與
進場裝機後，已於 07/2026 起正式進入量產階段，帶動欣銓
08/2026 營收增加至 17.94 億元， MoM+1.57% ， YoY+46.57% 。
此廠區主要與晶圓代工廠 (Foundry) 合作，針對  AI ASIC 提供
測試服務。這批高階機台目前的稼動率極高，幾乎處於滿載狀
態，其量產將顯著貢獻 3Q26 營收，也會讓晶圓代工業務的營收
佔比在 3Q26 逐漸放大。此外，在傳統應用領域如車用 IC 及  
General MCU 方面 ， 由於客戶端在疫後的庫存已降至合理水位 ，
需求正逐步回升。預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.73 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 21 日

2 群益投顧 
  欣銓 ESG ：  
欣銓積極推動  ESG （環境、社會、公司治理）相關政策與行動，並於官方
網站設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書，公開揭露永續發展成果與目
標。  
環境（ Environmental ）  
•持續推動再生能源與減碳：延續加入  RE100 的承諾，積極推動節能減碳
與再生能源採購，並簽署 SBTi （科學基礎減碳目標倡議）承諾，強化減碳
目標與國際接軌。  
•循環經濟新提案：攜手供應鏈夥伴推動循環箱再進化，落實資源再利用與
減廢。  
•生物多樣性與生態尊重：持續舉辦淨灘、認養友善田等活動，強調尊重生
態、友善健康。  
社會（ Social ）  
•同仁參與與文化深化：舉辦「 ESG Award 」專案競賽，鼓勵員工提出永續點
子與專案，涵蓋綠色製造、責任供應鏈、多元包容職場、人才培育、關懷弱
勢等五大方向。  
• 員工關懷與職場環境：持續改善員工宿舍環境，提升同仁生活品質。  
•社會公益：贊助在地傳統藝文活動、捐助弱勢團體，並與產業園區合作舉
辦移工籃球賽，促進多元共融。  
公司治理（ Governance ）  
•資訊透明與永續報告：發行永續報告書，持續自主揭露非財務資訊，強調
資訊透明與溝通成效。  
•國際認證與標準：完成 RBA VAP 8.0 改版訓練，強化人權、環保等國際標
準合規與超越法規要求。  
•永續獎項肯定：榮獲「英國標準協會（ BSI ）ESG 永續發展領航獎」及「台
灣企業永續獎（ TCSA ）－永續報告獎金獎」，肯定公司在永續發展、資訊
揭露與社會責任的表現。  
攜手合作夥伴擴大永續影響力，推動以大帶小、標竿學習。展現穩健落實永
續目標與全球標準接軌的實力。  
◼CDP 氣候問卷榮獲  A-List 最高肯定 ： 公司在2025 年  CDP 全球環境評
比中，從全球逾  22,100 家企業脫穎而出，「氣候變遷」問卷榮獲 A 級領
導等級，「水安全」蟬聯  A- 領導等級肯定。  
◼ SBTi 近程目標通過驗證：公司設定的近程減碳目標已於 04/2026 正式通
過科學基礎減量目標倡議（ SBTi ）官方驗證。  
◼連續  9 年榮獲「公司治理評鑑前  5% 」的殊榮。  
◼龍潭廠榮獲「 2026 台灣建築永續獎金獎」肯定。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

6693.TT 廣閎科 
 伺服器佔比持續攀升

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
廣閎科技是台灣功率元件與類比 IC 設計公司，核心產品包括 Power MOSFET 功率元
件、BLDC Motor Driver 無刷直流馬達驅動系統、SoC 散熱風扇驅動 IC，提供低碳、
低耗能的高能源效率產品，從 IC 到電機/電池/電源管理創新系統設計方案。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 233 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】無 
【大盤指數/股價】45849 / 184

➢ 伺服器應用佔比持續上升，2Q26 EPS 2.21 元，創歷史新高 
廣閎科 2Q26 營收 6.52 億元，QoQ+26.76%，YoY+102.24%，稅後淨
利 1.0 億元，QoQ+89.63%，YoY 轉虧為盈，EPS 2.21 元。工控車用及
消費性電子應用領域動能下滑，但伺服器相關功率元件需求強勁，廣閎
科在伺服器相關應用營收季成長 138%，產品毛利率相對高，且在漲價
帶動下，毛利率上升至 32.3%，季增 3.57 百分點。 
➢ 伺服器領域產品轉嫁能力強，2026 下半年營運逐季走高 
廣閎科 8 月營收 2.51 億元，MoM+3.61%，YoY+114.20%，目前受限
晶圓代工、下游封裝產能，營運動能增長的驅動力，來自投片產品線的
調整及價格的調漲，廣閎科透過降低消費性產品的投片，提高伺服器相
關產品的比重，能有效改善產品組合及獲利能力，雖然晶圓代工及封測
可能再調漲價格，但 48V BLDC total solution 滲透率持續提升，投研部
認為應用於伺服器的功率元件或晶片控制模組，轉嫁能力佳，能彌補消
費性電子的衰退。 
整體而言，廣閎科憑藉著在控制模組的整合能力，加上中壓功率元件市
場的台廠利基 ， 公司在AI 伺服器領域的成長毛利率及獲利能力仍具有提
升空間，獲利趨勢不變。投研部預估廣閎科 2H26 營運仍將呈現緩步走
高趨勢，預估 3Q26 營收 7.44 億元，QoQ+14.10%，YoY+109.71%，
稅後淨利 1.19 億元，QoQ+19.09%，YoY+316.35%，EPS 2.59 元。 
➢ 預估廣閎科 2027 年 EPS 11.13 元，建議買進，目標價 233 元 
投研部預估廣閎科 2026 年營收 26.68 億元，YoY+88.71%，稅後淨利
3.95 億元 ，YoY 轉虧為盈 ，EPS 8.69 元 ； 預估2027 年營收 30.38 億元 ，
YoY+13.86%，稅後淨利 5.09 億元，YoY+28.81%，EPS 11.13 元。以
2027 年 EPS 評價，目前廣閎科 PE 16X ，位於 PE 區間 10X~40X 中下
緣位置，考量 AI 伺服器領域成長動能佳，首次評等給予買進投資建議，
目標價 233 元(2027F P21X)。

6693 廣閎科 加權指數

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

公司簡介 
廣閎科技成立於 2007 年，是台灣功率元件與類比IC 設計公司，核心產品包括Power 
MOSFET 功率元件、BLDC Motor Driver 無刷直流馬達驅動系統、SoC 散熱風扇驅動
IC，提供低碳、低耗能的高能源效率產品，從 IC 到電機/電池/電源管理創新系統設計
方案。Power MOSFET 功率元件營收比重約 70%，SoC 散熱風扇驅動 IC 及 BLDC 
Motor Driver 無刷直流馬達驅動系統約佔 30%。就產品應用層面來看，2Q26 工控車
用佔 33%，伺服器相關佔 47%，消費性電子佔 20%。 
圖 1：廣閎科產品應用比重

資料來源：廣閎科，國票投顧 
伺服器應用佔比持續上升，2Q26 EPS 2.21 元，創歷史新高 
廣閎科 2Q26 營收 6.52 億元，QoQ+26.76%，YoY+102.24%，稅後淨利 1.0 億元，
QoQ+89.63%，YoY 轉虧為盈，EPS 2.21 元。工控車用及消費性電子應用領域動能下
滑，但伺服器相關功率元件需求強勁，廣閎科在伺服器相關應用營收季成長 138%，
產品毛利率相對高，且在漲價帶動下，毛利率上升至 32.3%，季增 3.57 百分點。 
聚焦伺服器領域功率元件及控制模組，趨勢向上 
展望未來，消費性電子受晶圓代工、封測及零組件漲價影響，消費動能疲弱；工控及
車用領域持平；AI 伺服器對功率元件需求則呈現強勁成長。在目前晶圓代工及封裝產
能滿載情況下，廣閎科透過產能調配，優化其產品組合及獲利能力。目前伺服器領域
佔廣閎科營收比重約 47%，其中應用於電源供應器相關約佔 17%，備援電池(BBU)約
佔 10%，散熱風扇控制模組佔 10%。 
(1) 電源供應器中壓 MOSFET，台廠利基所在 
在 AI 伺服器的電源供應器領域，前端高壓功率元件、主板上低壓的 DrMOS，仍
由歐美 IDM 廠主導 ， 台廠的利基點聚焦在中壓功率產品 。 目前廣閎科約有17%營
收 ， 來自AI 伺服器電源供應器二次側 、 及54V 轉 12V DC-DC 模組的中壓 MOSFET
產品 。 在AI 伺服器供應鏈中 ， 高壓功率元件與核心主板供電的低壓大電流DrMOS 
具備極高的技術壁壘，利潤佳， IDM 巨頭將產能聚焦於高壓第三代半導體以及
GPU 核心供電的低壓 DrMOS/SPS 晶片。 在HVDC 架構下 ，電源進入機櫃後 ，需
經過多級降壓 ，需使用 80V–100V 的 MOSFET 進行同步整流與開關控制， 提供系
統安全防護與電路隔離的 Hot-Swap MOSFET。

36% 32% 20%
13% 25% 47%
51% 43% 33%
2025 1Q26 2Q26
消費性電子 伺服器相關 工控車用

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
(2) 48V BLDC Motor Driver 滲透率持續拉升 
在 AI 伺服器散熱區塊 ， 廣閎科主要提供12V SoC 散熱風扇晶片 、48V BLDC Motor 
Driver 無刷直流馬達驅動控制模組，兩者佔營收比重約 10%，隨著 AI 伺服器運
算力大幅拉升，過大的電流常引發嚴重的電阻熱損耗，為了達成高效節能並優化
散熱，AI 運算中心大舉推進 48V 供電變革，高電壓環境對系統耐壓限制極高，使
得散熱驅動技術被迫轉型，不再採用高度微縮的 12V SoC 整合晶片，轉而全面導
入結合高壓驅動 IC 與分離式大電流功率元件的 48V 高規馬達控制模組 ， 目前48V 
BLDC Motor Driver 營收佔比已超過 12V SoC，廣閎科憑藉著過往在白色家電領
域所積累的 BLDC 馬達驅動與功率元件開發經驗，成功切入伺服器 48V BLDC 領
域，其將 MCU、驅動 IC 與 MOSFET 預先整合於單一驅動模組板出貨，提供隨插
即用的一站式方案，免除散熱大廠硬體電路調校與訊號匹配，能縮短 AI 伺服器供
應鏈的開案時程，協助客戶快速通過嚴格的系統驗證。 
48V BLDC Motor Driver 相較於 12 V SoC ，需搭載 6 顆獨立的高壓低阻抗  
MOSFET，單價較對 12V 高，且在 48V 散熱模組的供電前端，必須搭配一組負責
熱插拔控制與電路保護的 MOSFET，廣閎科能透過 48CV BLDC Motor Driver 的
供貨優勢，能順勢搶食前端 Hot Swap MOSFET 市場。 
圖 2：SoC 散熱風扇驅動 IC 圖 3：伺服器散熱 4028 驅動板(48V/1A/FOC)
